## ०१

<div class="js_include" includetitle="false" newlevelforh1="3" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarva-prastutiH/02/02/3/01_varuNo_hainadrAjyakAma_Adadhe.md">
<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

व᳓रुणो हैनद्राज्य᳓काम आ᳓दधे᳓ ।  
स᳓ राज्य᳓मगछत् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

1. Now Varuṇa established this (fire), being desirous of sovereignty. He obtained sovereignty; and, accordingly, whether one (who has established the fires) knows (this) or not, they call him 'king Varuṇa.'
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Varuṇa indeed, established this (fire) being desirous of sovereignty. He attained sovereignty. Therefore, he who knows this and he who does not, all say, "Varuṇa is the King".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

व्व᳘रुणो हैनद्राज्य᳘काम ऽआ᳘दधे॥  
स᳘ राज्य᳘मगच्छत्
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

व᳘रुणो हैनद्राज्य᳘काम आ᳘दधे॥  
स राज्य᳘मगछत्
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

**अथ पुनराधानम् ।** 

वरुणो हैनद् राज्यकाम आदधे । स राज्यमागच्छत् ।
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

व᳘रुणो हैनन्द्राज्य᳘काम आ᳘दधे स᳘ राज्य᳘मगच्छत्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/39 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓स्माद्य᳓श्चैत᳓द्वे᳓द य᳓श्च न᳓ व᳓रुणो रा᳓जे᳓त्येवा᳓चक्षते सो᳓मो य᳓शस्कामः स᳓ य᳓शो ऽभवत्त᳓स्माद्ये᳓ च सो᳓मे ल᳓भन्ते [[ये|ये᳓]] च न᳓ ल᳓भन्त उभ᳓य एवै᳙नदा᳓गच्छन्ति य᳓श एवै᳙त᳓द्द्र᳓ष्टुमा᳓गच्छन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Soma was desirous of glory. (He established this fire). He became glorious. Therefore those who acquire (in the matter of) Soma and those who don't, both of them acquire this (glory) verily.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्माद्य᳘श्चैतद्वे᳘द य᳘श्च न व᳘रुणो राजे᳘त्येवा᳘चक्षते सो᳘मो य᳘शस्कामः स य᳘शो ऽभवत्त᳘स्माद्ये᳘ च सो᳘मे ल᳘भन्ते ये च न ल᳘भन्त उभ᳘य ए᳘वैनदा᳘गच्छन्ति य᳘श ए᳘वैतद्द्र᳘ष्टुमा᳘गच्छन्ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

य᳓शो ह भव᳓ति राज्यं᳓ गच्छति य᳓ एनदेवं᳓ विद्वा᳓नाध᳓त्ते॥

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/02/02/3/03_yasho_ha_bhavati.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

Glorious therefore he becomes, and sovereignty he obtains, whosoever, knowing this, establishes a sacrificial fire of his own.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

People do come to see the glorious. He who, knowing this is so, sets up (the fires), indeed, becomes glorious and attains sovereignty.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

य᳘शो ह भवति राज्यं᳘ गच्छति य᳘ ऽएवं᳘ व्विद्वा᳘नाध᳘त्ते॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

य᳘शो ह भवति राज्यं᳘ गछति य᳘ एवं᳘ विद्वा᳘नाधत्ते॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

यशो ह भवति, राज्यं गच्छति- य एवं विद्वानाधत्ते ॥ १ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

य᳘शो ह भव᳘ति राज्यं᳘ गच्छति य᳘ एनदेवं᳘ विद्वा᳘नाध᳘त्ते ॥१॥
</details>

## ०२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/33 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अग्ना᳓ उ ह वै᳓ देवाः᳓ स᳓र्वाणि रूपा᳓णि संनि᳓दधिरे या᳓नि च ग्राम्या᳓णि या᳓नि चारण्या᳓नि विजयं᳓ वोपपरैष्य᳓न्तः कामचार᳓स्य वा का᳓मायायं᳓ नो गो᳓पिष्ठो गोपायत्वि᳓ति वा [[तान्ह्या᳓ग्निर्नि᳓चकमे|ता᳓न्ह्या᳓ग्निर्नि᳓चकमे]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The Gods' deposited, once, all forms --(things and forms), those that were rural and those that were of the forest (domestic and wild), either because they (the gods) were going out to achieve victory (in battle against the enemies) or with a desire for free activity or with the thought, "This (Agni) is the best protector and may he protect us". Agni, indeed, coveted them much. Gathering them (the forms) he entered the seasons. The gods thought, "Let us go back" and came to (the place of) Agni who had disappeared.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अग्ना᳘ उ ह वै᳘ देवाः स᳘र्वाणि रूपा᳘णि संनि᳘दधिरे या᳘नि च ग्राम्या᳘णि या᳘नि चारण्या᳘नि विजयं᳘ वोपपरैष्य᳘न्तः कामचार᳘स्य वा का᳘मायायं᳘ नो गो᳘पिष्ठो गोपायत्वि᳘ति वा ता᳘न्ह्याग्निर्नि᳘चकमे
</details>

## ०३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓तो हैत᳓त्त्व᳓ष्टा पुनराधे᳓यं ददर्श तदा᳓दधे᳓ यो᳙ऽग्नेः᳓ प्रियं᳓ धा᳓मो᳓पजगाम त᳓स्मा एता᳓न्यग्नि᳓रुभ᳓यानि रूपा᳓णि प्रति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then, indeed, did Tvaṣṭā see (intuitively discovered) this, 'Re-installation' (re-installed fire). He established it and he reached Agni's favourite abode. Agni gave out to him both these forms, the domestic and the wild.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘तो हैतत्त्व᳘ष्टा पुनराधे᳘यं ददर्श तदा᳘दधे योऽग्नेः᳘ प्रियं धामो᳘पजगाम त᳘स्मा एता᳘न्यग्नि᳘रुभ᳘यानि रूपा᳘णि प्रति
</details>

## ०४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓स्मै कं पुनराधे᳓यमा᳓दधीत त᳓था हैवा᳙ग्नेः᳓ प्रियं᳓ धा᳓मो᳓पगच्छति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

So one should set up a re-installed fire for him (Tvaṣṭṛ)?
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/19 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓स्मा एता᳓न्यग्नि᳓रुभ᳓यानि [[रू᳓पाणि|रूपा᳓णि]] प्रतिनिः᳓सृजति या᳓नि च ग्राम्या᳓णि या᳓नि चारण्या᳓नि त᳓स्मिन्नेता᳓न्युभ᳓यानि रूपा᳓णि दृश्यन्ते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Thus, indeed, one reaches the favourite abode of Agni. To him, Agni re-delivers both these forms that are domestic and that are wild. In it (the fire) are seen both these forms. That is indeed the supreme state. They (people) like him. So does he flourish.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्मा एता᳘न्यग्नि᳘रुभ᳘यानि रू᳘पाणि प्रतिनिः᳘सृजति या᳘नि च ग्राम्या᳘णि या᳘नि चारण्या᳘नि त᳘स्मिन्नेता᳘न्युभ᳘यानि रूपा᳘णि दृश्यन्ते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

वर्षा᳓ वै᳓ स᳓र्व ऋत᳓वो वर्षा᳓ हि᳓ वै᳓ स᳓र्व ऋत᳓वो᳓ऽथादो᳓ वर्ष᳓मकुर्मादो᳓ वर्ष᳓मकुर्मे᳓ति वर्षा᳓भिरेव᳓ संवत्सरा᳓न्त्सं᳓पश्यन्ति ।
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/02/02/3/22_varShA_vai_sarva.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

The rains are all the seasons, for the rains are indeed all the seasons: hence, in counting over years, people say, 'In such and such a year (or rain, varsha) we did it; in such and such a year (or rain) we did it.' The rains, then, are one of the forms of manifestation (rūpa) of all seasons [^egg_711]; and when people say, 'To-day it is as if in summer,' then that is in the rainy season; and when they say, 'To-day it is as if in spring,' then that, too, is in the rainy season.

[^egg_711]: 315:2 This speculation is based on the identity of the words for year (varsha; also 'rain') and the rains, or rainy season (varshāḥ).
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed the rainy season (itself) is all the seasons. Verily is the rainy season all the seasons. Hence, they consider (count) the years only with the rainy season, saying "We did (this) for a year (in that year) ", "We did this that year," for the rainy season is all the seasons. And therefore the rainy season itself is the form of all the seasons.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

व्वर्षा वै स᳘र्व्व ऽऋत᳘वो व्वर्षा हि वै स᳘र्व्व ऽऋतवो᳘ ऽथादो᳘ व्वर्ष᳘मकुर्मादो᳘ व्वर्ष᳘मकुर्मे᳘ति संव्वत्सरान्त्स᳘म्पश्यन्ति
</details>

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

तस्य पुनराधेयस्य त्रैवर्णिकसाधारण्येन ऋतुविशेषं विधत्ते [^१_४७]- **स वा** इति ।

ऋत्वन्तरं परित्यज्य वर्षर्तुविधाने [^२_४७] कारणमाह- **वर्षा वा** इति ।

एतदुपपादयति- **वर्षा हि वा** इत्यादिना ।

यस्मादेवं वर्षर्तुः सर्वात्मकः, तस्मात् समुदायात्मकं संवत्सरं वर्षशब्देन लोको व्यवहरतीति दर्शयति- **अथे**ति ।

[^१_४७]: "वर्षासु मध्यंदिने वा" का० श्रौ० सू० ४. २६४ ।

[^२_४७]: 'वर्षर्त्तावेव विधाने' इति क्व. ।
</details>
</details>
</div>

## ०६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

भ᳓वति वै᳓ त᳓द्वर्षा᳓सु य᳓त्राहु᳓र्ग्रीष्म᳓ एवा᳙द्ये᳓ति भ᳓वति त᳓द्य᳓त्राहुः᳓ शि᳓शिर एवा᳙द्ये᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed, it happens in the rainy season when they say, "Today is verily summer!" It happens in the rainy season when they say, "Today is verily winter!" From the rain (it goes) into the years, indeed.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

भ᳘वति वै त᳘द्वर्षा᳘सु य᳘त्राहु᳘र्ग्रीष्म᳘ ए᳘वाद्ये᳘ति भ᳘वति तद्य᳘त्राहुः शि᳘शिर ए᳘वाद्ये᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/41 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

वर्षा᳓दि᳓दु [[वर्षेष्वथै᳓तत्परो᳓क्षमिव|वर्षे᳓ष्व᳓थैत᳓त्परो᳓क्षमिव]] रूपं᳓ य᳓देव᳓ पुर᳓स्ताद्वा᳓ति [[तद्वसन्त᳓स्य|त᳓द्वसन्त᳓स्य]] रूपं᳓ य᳓त्स्तन᳓यति त᳓द्ग्रीष्म᳓स्य य᳓द्व᳓र्षति त᳓द्वर्षा᳓णां य᳓द्विद्यो᳓तते त᳓च्छर᳓दो य᳓द्वृष्टो᳙द्गृह्णा᳓ति त᳓द्धेमन्त᳓स्य वर्षाः᳓ स᳓र्व ऋत᳓व ऋतू᳓न्प्रा᳓विशत् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

And besides, this is, as it were, (the year's) invisible form. What blows in the east, that itself is the form of spring; what thunders is of summer, what rains is of the rainy season, what flashes (lightning) is of autumn and what, having rained sucks it up is of the season of snow.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

वर्षादि᳘दु वर्षैष्वथै᳘ तत्परो᳘क्षमिव रूपं य᳘देव᳘ पुर᳘स्ताद्वा᳘ति तद्वसन्त᳘स्य रूपं य᳘त्स्तन᳘यति त᳘द्ग्रीष्म᳘स्य यद्व᳘र्षति त᳘द्वर्षा᳘णां य᳘द्विद्यो᳘तते त᳘च्छर᳘दो य᳘द्वृ᳘ष्टोद्गृह्णा᳘ति त᳘द्धेमन्त᳘स्य वर्षाः स᳘र्व ऋत᳘व ऋतून्प्रा᳘विशत्
</details>

## ०७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/29 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[आदित्यो|आदित्यो᳓]] वाव᳓ स᳓र्व ऋत᳓वः स᳓ य᳓दैवो᳙दे᳓त्य᳓थ वसन्तो᳓ यदा᳓ संगवो᳓ ऽथ ग्रीष्मो᳓ यदा᳓ मध्य᳓न्दिनो᳓ऽथ वर्षा᳓ यदा᳓ न्यह्नो᳙ऽपराह्णो᳓ऽथ शर᳓द्यदा᳙स्त᳓मे᳓त्य᳓थ हेमन्तः᳓॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed, the Sun is all the seasons. When it rises, even then is it spring; when it is the time for being gathered together (for milking) it is summer; when it is mid-day it is the rainy season; when the day declines, i.e. when it is afternoon, it is autumn. When he sets, then it is the season of snow (winter).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

आदित्यो वाव स᳘र्व ऋत᳘वः स य᳘दै᳘वोदेत्य᳘थ वसन्तो᳘ यदा᳘ संगवो᳘ ऽथ ग्रीष्मो᳘ यदा᳘ मध्यं᳘ न्दिनो᳘ऽथ वर्षा᳘ यदा᳘ न्य᳘ह्नोऽपराह्णो᳘ऽथ शर᳘द्य᳘दास्तमेत्य᳘थ हेमन्तः॥७॥
</details>

## ०९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ वै᳓ कुशै᳓रु᳓द्धरति दा᳓रुभिर्वा᳓ अ᳓ग्न उ᳓द्धरति दा᳓रुभिर᳓ग्ने दा᳓रुभिर᳓परं जामि᳓ कुर्यात्सम᳓दं कुर्यात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Verily he takes it (fire) up by means of sacrificial grass. Or he takes it out with (pieces of) wood, the first time. (Should he take the fire) first with (pieces of) wood and the second time (too) with (pieces of ) wood, he would be committing repetition and he would be doing it with arrogance.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स वै᳘ कुशैरु᳘द्धरति दा᳘रुभिर्वा अ᳘ग्न उ᳘द्धरति दा᳘रुभिर᳘ग्ने दा᳘रुभिर᳘परं जामि᳘ कुर्यात्सम᳘दं कुर्यात्
</details>

## १०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/16 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[सो᳙ऽर्कपलाशशाखाभ्यां|सो᳙ऽर्कपलाशशाखा᳓भ्यां]] व्रीहिम᳓यमपूपं᳓ कृत्वा᳓ य᳓त्र गा᳓र्हपत्यमाधास्य᳓न्भ᳓वति त᳓न्नि᳓दधाति [[त᳓द्गा᳓र्हपत्यमभ्य᳓दधाति|त᳓द्गा᳓र्हपत्यमभ्य᳙दधाति]]॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/02/02/3/37_arkapalAshAbhyAM_vrIhimayamapUpaM_kRtvA.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

12. Having prepared an (ordinary) rice cake on

two arka [^egg_714] leaves, he puts it in the place where he is about to establish the Gārhapatya fire, and thereon lays down the Gārhapatya.

[^egg_714]: 317:1 Calotropis Gigantea. These cakes (apūpa, not puroḍāśa, have first to be cooked either on the Avasathya, or on a secular fire. Before the cakes are then put on the Gārhapatya and Āhavanīya fire-places, the latter have to be consecrated in the usual way (cf. p. 2); and, after the putting on of the cakes, the fire-places are sprinkled by the Adhvaryu, while the sacrificer holds on to him from behind. Katy. IV, 11, 8, Schol.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having prepared a cake of rice with two leaves of the arha plant, he places it at the spot where he is going to establish the Gārhapatya. And then he lays down the Gārhapatya there.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य) अर्क्कपलाशा᳘भ्याम्॥  
व्रीहिम᳘यमपू᳘पं कृत्वा य᳘त्र गा᳘र्हपत्यमाधास्यन्भ᳘वति तन्नि᳘दधाति तद्गा᳘र्हपत्यमा᳘दधाति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अर्कपलाशा᳘भ्यां॥  
व्रीहिम᳘यमपू᳘पं कृत्वा य᳘त्र गार्हपत्यमाधास्यन्भ᳘वति तन्नि᳘दधाति तद्गा᳘र्हपत्यमादधाति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अर्कपलाशाभ्यां व्रीहिमयमपूपं कृत्वा यत्र गार्हपत्यमाधास्यन् भवति, तन्निदधाति; तद् गार्हपत्यमादधाति ॥ १२ ॥
</details>

## १२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓दु ना᳓द्रियेत रा᳓त्रिभिर्वाव᳓ सो᳙ ऽन्त᳓र्हितो᳓ ऽथैत᳓माग्नेयं᳓ प᳓ञ्चकपालं पुरोल्ला᳓शं नि᳓र्वपति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

One need not favour that (practice), for it becomes covered indeed by the nights. Then he offers this oblation (purodāśa) cooked in five potsherds and dedicated to Agni.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दु ना᳘द्रियेत रा᳘त्रिभिर्वाव᳘ सो ऽन्त᳘र्हितो᳘ ऽथैत᳘माग्नेयं प᳘ञ्चकपालं पुरोल्ला᳘शं नि᳘र्वपति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓स्य प᳓ञ्चपदाः पङ्क्त᳓यो याज्यानुवाक्या᳙ भवन्ति प᳓ञ्चर्त᳓व ऋतू᳓न्प्रा᳓विशदृतु᳓भ्य एवै᳙नमेत᳓न्नि᳓र्मिमीते॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

For it, the offering mantras and (connected) subsequent utterances (Yājyās and anuvākyās) are in Panikti (metre) with five pādas (feet); for, the seasons are five. He (Agni) entered the seasons. He (the sacrificer) thus produces this (fire) from the very seasons.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्य प᳘ञ्चपदाः पङ्क्त᳘यो याज्यानुवा᳘क्या भवन्ति प᳘ञ्चर्त᳘व ऋतूनप्रा᳘विशदृतु᳘भ्य ए᳘वैनमेतन्नि᳘र्मिमीते ॥१२॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The other sacrifice belongs to all the gods. But this that belongs to Agni is special. The last ‘subsequent offering’ he (the sacrificer) offers in a loud voice. Then, indeed, he becomes, as it were, one who has done his work.
</details>

## १४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/23 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ [[य᳓त्राध्वर्यु᳓रतिक्र᳓म्याश्राव्या᳓ह|य᳓त्राध्वर्यु᳓रतिक्र᳓म्याश्रा᳓व्या᳓ह]] समि᳓धो य᳓जे᳓त्याग्नेय᳓मेव᳓ त᳓द्रूपं᳓ परो᳓क्षमिव त्व᳙ग्नी᳓न्यजे᳓ति हैव᳓ [[तत्र᳓|त᳓त्र]] ब्रूयात्॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When the Adhvaryu, having stepped beyond and having called (drawn attention) says, “Worship (offer) (pronounce the offering prayer for) ‘Samidhs’, it is, indeed, the form of Agni and it appears to be indirect. There one should say (directly) only “Worship the Agnis”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स य᳘त्राध्वर्यु᳘रतिक्र᳘म्याश्राव्याह समि᳘धो यजे᳘त्याग्नेय᳘मेव त᳘द्रूपं᳘ परो᳘क्षमिव᳘ त्वग्नी᳘न्यजे᳘ति हैव तत्र᳘ ब्रूयात्॥१४॥
</details>

## १६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ स्वा᳓हाग्निं᳓ स्वा᳓हाग्निं᳓ प᳓वमानमि᳓ति य᳓द्यग्न᳓ये प᳓वमानाय ध्रिये᳓रन्त्स्वा᳓हाग्नि᳓मि᳓न्दुमन्तमि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he says, “Svāhāgnim”. “Svāhāgnim Pavamānam”. If they decide on (offering it to) Agni Pavamāna. If they decide on (offering it to) Agni Indumat – (he says) “Svāhāgnim indumantam”. He (the sacrificer) makes all these principal sacrificial ceremonies dedicated to Agni, (uttering) “Svāhāgnim-ajyupām” (Svāhā; the fires that consume ghee) “May the Agnis, drinkers of ghee, coming together (being pleased), accept the ghee”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ स्वा᳘हाग्निंꣳ स्वा᳘हाग्निं प᳘वमानमि᳘ति य᳘द्यग्न᳘ये प᳘वमानाय ध्रिये᳘रन्त्स्वा᳘हाग्निमि᳘न्दुमन्तमि᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः)</summary>

य᳓द्यग्न᳓य इ᳓न्दुमते ध्रिये᳓रन्त्स्वा᳓हाग्नी᳓नाज्यपा᳓न्जुषाणा᳓ अग्न᳓य आज्यपा᳓ आ᳓ज्यस्य व्यन्तु [[वौषळि᳓त्येव᳓मेता᳓न्प्रयाजा᳓न्त्स᳓र्वानाग्नेया᳓न्करोति|वौ᳓षळि᳓त्येव᳓मेता᳓न्प्रयाजा᳓न्त्स᳓र्वानाग्नेया᳓न्करोति]]॥

“Vauṣat”.

## १७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/21 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थाहाग्न᳓ये᳓ [[ऽनुब्रूहीत्याग्नेय᳓मा᳓ज्यभागं|ऽनुब्रूही᳓त्याग्नेय᳓मा᳓ज्यभागं]] सो᳓ऽन्वाहाग्निं᳓ स्तो᳓मेन बोधय समिधानो᳓ अ᳓मर्त्यम् ।  
हव्या᳓ देवे᳓षु नो [[दधदि᳓ति|द᳓धदि᳓ति]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he says, “Pronounce the inviting prayer for Agni”, with regard to Agni’s portion of ghee. He (the Hoṭṛ) recites (following this instruction), “Wake up Agni” with praise, enkindling him the immortal, so that he takes our offerings to the gods”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थाहाग्नये᳘ ऽनुब्रूहीत्याग्नेयमा᳘ज्यभागंꣳ सो᳘ऽन्वाहाग्निꣳ स्तो᳘मेन बोधय समिधानो अ᳘मर्त्यम् । हव्या᳘ देवे᳘षु नो दधदि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स्व᳓पितीव खिद्वा᳓ एत᳓द्य᳓दु᳓द्वासितो भ᳓वति बोध᳓यत्येवै᳙नमेत᳓त्समु᳓द्दीपयति जुषाणो᳓ अग्नि᳓रा᳓ज्यस्य वेत्वि᳓ति यजति॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/02/02/3/56_svapitIva_khalu_vA.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

For, indeed, when Agni is removed (from the hearth) [^egg_726], he, as it were, sleeps: he (the priest) now awakens, rouses

him. For the offering-prayer he recites, 'May Agni graciously accept of the butter!'

[^egg_726]: 319:7 The fire laid down at the ādheya is removed when the punarādheya is to be performed.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed, having been removed, he (Agni) appears to sleep. He wakes him thus and makes him blaze up. He makes the offering, saying “May Agni be pleased and may he accept the ghee”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

स्वपितीव ख᳘लु वा᳘ ऽएतद्यदु᳘द्वासितो भ᳘वति सम्प्र᳘बोधयत्येवैनमेत᳘त्समु᳘दीर्ययति जुषाणो᳘ अग्निरा᳘ज्यस्य व्वेत्वि᳘ति यजति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

स्व᳘पितीव ख᳘लु वा᳘ एतद्यदु᳘द्वासितो भ᳘वति सम्प्र᳘बोधयत्येवैन᳘मेत᳘त्समु᳘दीर्ययति जुषाणो᳘ अग्निरा᳘ज्यस्य वेत्वि᳘ति यजति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

(ऋ. सं. मं. ५ सू. १४. ऋ. १ । वा. स. अ. २२ मं. १५) स्वपितीव खलु वा एतद् यदुद्वासितो भवति । **सम्प्रबोधयत्येवैनमेतत्समुदीर्ययति**- **"जुषाणो अग्निराज्यस्य वेतु"**- इति यजति ॥ २१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

स्व᳘पितीव खिद्वा᳘ एतद्यदु᳘द्वासितो भ᳘वति बोध᳘यत्ये᳘वैनमेत᳘त्समु᳘द्दीपयति जुषाणो᳘ अग्निरा᳘ज्यस्य वेत्वि᳘ति यजति ॥१७॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

तथा᳓हाग्नेयो᳓ भ᳓वति सो᳓मो वै᳓ [[प᳓वमानो᳓|प᳓वमानो]] [[नो|नो᳓]] सौम्या᳓दा᳓ज्यभागाद्यन्ति जुषाणो᳓ अग्निः᳓ प᳓वमान आ᳓ज्यस्य वेत्वि᳓ति यजति॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/02/02/3/58_tathAhAgneyo_bhavati_somo.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Thus indeed, it becomes of Agni. Soma indeed is the Pavamāna (the purifier). (The Soma elements) do not go away from the ghee portion belonging to Soma. "May Agni-Pavamāna being pleased, accept the ghee!" So he recites (on making the offering).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

तथा᳘हाग्नेयो भ᳘वति सो᳘मो वै प᳘वमानस्त᳘दु सौम्यादा᳘ज्यभागान्न᳘यन्ति जुषाणो᳘ ऽअग्निः प᳘वमान ऽआ᳘ज्यस्य व्वेत्वि᳘ति यजति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

तथा᳘हाग्नेयो भ᳘वति सो᳘मो वै प᳘वमानस्त᳘दु सौम्यादा᳘ज्यभागान्न᳘यन्ति जुषाणो᳘ अग्निः प᳘वमान आ᳘ज्यस्य वेत्वि᳘ति यजति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

तथाहाग्नेयो भवति । सोमो वै पमानः । तदु सौम्यादाज्यभागान्नयन्ति- **"जुषाणो ऽअग्निः पवमान आज्यस्य वेतु"** इति यजति ॥ २२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

तथा᳘हाग्नेयो भ᳘वति सो᳘मो वै प᳘वमानो᳘ नो सौम्यादा᳘ज्यभागाद्यन्ति जुषाणो᳘ अग्निः प᳘वमान आ᳘ज्यस्य वेत्वि᳘ति यजति ॥१८॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

तथा᳓हाग्नेयो᳓ भ᳓वति सो᳓मो वा᳓ [[इ᳓न्दु᳓र्नौ|इ᳓न्दुर्नौ]] सौम्या᳓दा᳓ज्यभागाद्यन्ति जुषाणो᳓ अग्नि᳓रि᳓न्दुमाना᳓ज्यस्य वेत्वि᳓ति यजति॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (Hotā) says, following this, "Come here, Oh Agni! I shall gladly say these and other words (of praise). May you grow with these drops." Then, indeed does it become of (dedicated to) Agni. Soma, indeed, is Indu (the drop). They (the Soma elements) do not go away from the portion of ghee belonging to Soma. He recites, in worship, "May Agni-Indumat (possessor of drops), being pleased, accept the ghee".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तथा᳘हाग्नेयो भ᳘वति सो᳘मो वा इ᳘न्दु᳘र्नौ सौम्यादा᳘ज्य- भागाद्यन्ति जुषाणो᳘ अग्निरि᳘न्दुमाना᳘ज्यस्य वेत्वि᳘ति यजति ॥१९॥
</details>

## २०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थाहाग्न᳓ये᳓ ऽनुब्रूही᳓त्यग्निं᳓ यजे᳓त्यग्न᳓ये स्विष्टकृ᳓ते᳓ ऽनुब्रूह्यग्निं᳓ स्विष्टकृ᳓तं य᳓जे᳓ति॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he says, "Make the call for Agni". "Worship (recite the offering prayers to) Agni". "Make the call for Agni Sviṣṭakṛt. (who makes the offerings well offered)". "Recite the offering prayers to Agni Sviṣṭakṛt".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थाहाग्नये᳘ ऽनुब्रूही᳘त्यग्निं᳘ यजे᳘त्यग्न᳘ये स्विष्टकृते᳘ ऽनुब्रूहीग्नि᳘ꣳ᳘ स्विष्टकृ᳘तं यजे᳘ति॥२०॥
</details>

## २१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ य᳓त्राध्वर्यु᳓रातिक्रा᳓म्याश्रा᳓व्या᳓ह देवा᳓न्यजे᳓त्यग्नी᳓द्य᳓जे᳓ति हैव᳓ त᳓त्र ब्रूयात्॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Where the Adhvaryu (priest) over-stepping (these) and calling attention says "Worship (recite the prayers) for the gods," there he should verily say (recite the prayers for) "worship Agnis".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स य᳘त्राध्वर्यु᳘रातिक्रा᳘म्याश्राव्या᳘ह देवा᳘न्यजे᳘त्यग्नीद्यजे᳘ति हैव त᳘त्र ब्रूयात्॥२१॥

## २२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ यजत्यग्ने᳓र्वसुव᳓ने वसुधेय᳓स्य वेतु वौ᳓षळि᳓त्यग्ना᳓ उ वसुव᳓ने वसुधे᳓यस्य वेतु वौ᳓षळि᳓ति देवो᳓ अग्निः᳓ स्विष्टकृ᳓दि᳓ति स्वय᳓मग्नेय᳓स्तृती᳓यो ऽनुयाजः᳓॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He recites "May it (Barhis) accept (the offering) for Agni re-acquisition of abundant wealth": "Vauṣat". "The divine Agni Sviṣṭakṛt". This third subsequent offering by itself belongs to Agni.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स᳘ यजत्यग्ने᳘र्वसुव᳘ने वसुधेय᳘स्य वेतु वौ᳘षळि᳘त्यग्ना᳘ उ वसुव᳘ने वसुधे᳘यस्य वेतु वौ᳘षळि᳘ति देवो᳘ अग्निः᳘ स्विष्टकृदि᳘ति स्वय᳘मग्नेय᳘स्तृती᳘यो ऽनुयाजः॥२२॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ता᳓सां षण्णां᳓ वि᳓भक्तीनां द्वा᳓दश वा त्र᳓योदश वाक्ष᳓राणि द्वा᳓दश वा वै᳓ त्र᳓योदश वा संवत्सर᳓स्य मा᳓साः संवत्सर᳓ ऋतू᳓न्प्रा᳓विशत् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

These are indeed the six case-forms. Four (are used) in primary offerings and two in after-offerings. Verily six are the seasons. He (Agni) entered the seasons. He, thus, produces him (Agni) only from the seasons. Of the six case-forms, there are twelve or thirteen syllables. Indeed twelve or thirteen are the months of the year. The year entered the seasons. He (Agni) entered the year, the seasons. He verily produces him (Agni) from the seasons. He does not put two together.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ता᳘साꣳ षण्णां वि᳘भक्तीनां द्वा᳘दश वा त्र᳘योदश वाक्ष᳘राणि द्वा᳘दश वा वै त्र᳘योदश वा संवत्सर᳘स्य मा᳘साः संवत्सर᳘ ऋतून्प्रा᳘विशत्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

जामि᳓ हि᳓ कुर्या᳓द्य᳓द्द्वे᳓ चित्सह᳓ [[कुर्या᳓द्विष्वग्यन्त्वि᳓ति|कुर्या᳓द्वि᳓ष्वग्यन्त्वि᳓ति]] प्रयाजा᳓नां रूपं᳓ वसुवने᳓ वसुधे᳓यस्य वेत्वि᳓ति ।

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

"May they accept". The form of (expression) after-offerings is "May it accept for abundant acquisition of riches" Gold is the sacrificial fee.
</details>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/27 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अनुयाजा᳓नां हि᳓रण्यं द᳓क्षिणाग्नेयो᳙ऽयं᳓ यज्ञो᳙ ऽग्ने᳓र्हि᳓ रे᳓तो हि᳓रण्यमनड्वा᳓ननड्वा᳓न्हि᳓ [[व᳓हसाग्ने᳓यो᳙|व᳓हसाग्नेयो᳙]] [[ऽन्यवदर्धैमिव|ऽन्यवदर्धै᳓मिव]] ह्य᳙नळु᳓हो व᳓हो भ᳓वति देवा᳓नां हव्यवा᳓हनो ऽग्नि᳓र्वा᳓हत्येष᳓ मनुष्ये᳙भ्यः॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This sacrifice verily belongs to Agni. Indeed gold is the seed of Agni. Or it (the sacrificial fee) is an ox. Verily the ox is related to Agni, by its shoulder. The shoulder of the ox looks as if it is burnt by fire. Agni is the carrier of the oblations to the gods. This ox carries (burdens) for the sake of men.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अनुयाजा᳘नाꣳ हि᳘रण्यं द᳘क्षिणाग्ने᳘यो᳘ऽयं᳘ य᳘ज्ञो ऽग्नेर्हि रे᳘तो हि᳘रण्यम- नड्वा᳘ननड्वान्हि व᳘हसाग्ने᳘यो᳘ ऽन्यवदर्धैमिव᳘ ह्यनळु᳘हो व᳘हो भ᳘वति देवा᳘नाꣳ हव्यवा᳘हनो ऽग्निर्वा᳘हत्येष᳘ मनु᳘ष्येभ्यः ॥२३॥
</details>
